- [x] **GR-RESID-IROH** ✅ DONE (2026-06-21) — iroh peer-auth + audit + shared state (D3/F19/F56), built + verified under `--features cluster-iroh` (3 tests pass + full lib 8575 compiles with the feature; all changes feature-gated → default build untouched). **(D3 peer-auth)** the iroh accept path now requires a `cluster_key` HMAC Hello proof: `IrohTransport::bind` takes `Option<Arc<ClusterKey>>`; the dial side (`send_frame`) prepends a 32-byte `compute_cluster_key_proof(key, our_id, peer_id)` (signer-first, reflection-safe — `cluster::peer_auth`); `GossipProtocol::accept` splits the proof prefix + `verify_peer_proof(.., peer=remote_id, own=our_id)` BEFORE `peers.insert`/before the handler runs — a peer that reaches the ALPN but can't prove membership is dropped (the iroh EndpointId IS an ed25519 key, so the proof binds the transport identity to the shared cluster_key). `serve.rs` now threads the `cluster_key` from `cluster_transport_activation` (was discarded). **(F19 audit)** `gossip_handler` takes `Option<Arc<WalWriterHandle>>` + emits `0xEE CLUSTER_GOSSIP_RECEIVED` (accept) / `0xEF CLUSTER_GOSSIP_DROPPED` (reject/malformed/peer-auth-fail, carries reason) via `try_append_sync` (sync, works in the sync FrameHandler) — peeroxide-parity WAL coverage on the receive path. **(F56 shared state)** `spawn_gossip_broadcast` now takes the SAME `Arc<Mutex<GossipState>>` the handler uses (VC + dedup frontier converge across send+receive; std guard scoped to drop before the broadcast await) + `self_id = PeerPubkey::new(transport.node_id())` (the real iroh transport identity, not a per-process random uuid). Send-side gossip audit (`0xED CLUSTER_GOSSIP_SENT`) on the iroh broadcast also shipped (2026-06-21 follow-up) — `spawn_gossip_broadcast` takes the writer + emits `0xED` (frame_count/delivered/peer_count) per non-empty tick via `try_append_sync`, full F19 parity (receive 0xEE/0xEF + send 0xED) with the peeroxide path. +1 test. *files:* `cluster/iroh_transport.rs`, `cli/serve.rs`. — *origin:* review D3/F19/F56 (operator: "keine primitives → ins gold md").
- [x] **GR-RESID-D34** ✅ DONE (2026-06-21) — robust daemon-minted token replaces flag-trust. **`--gui-confirmed` alone NO LONGER bypasses** the FULL-AUTO TTY gate — `confirm_full_auto(pre_confirmed, token_ok)` now requires `token_ok`, else it bails (closes the "bake the flag into a cron" accident vector). New `audit_rpc::FullAutoTokenStore` (single-use, 120s-TTL, constant-time match, fail-closed RNG, re-mint replaces prior) on `AuditRpcState`; two new auth-gated loopback endpoints on the existing audit-RPC server — `POST /fullauto-token/mint` (→ `{token}`) + `POST /fullauto-token/consume` (→ 200/401, single-use); client `mint_fullauto_token`/`consume_fullauto_token`. `run_set_mode` consumes `--gui-token` against the daemon before allowing the bypass. Hidden CLI `neoth autonomy mint-fullauto-token` (prints the nonce, needs a live daemon) — the GUI runs it after its confirm dialog, captures the token, then spawns `full-auto --gui-confirmed --gui-token <t>` (mint→use is a live GUI+daemon sequence, not a static flag). The 0xDD audit still fires. **Residual (single-user reality):** a same-uid attacker who reads the audit-RPC bearer could mint+consume live — fundamentally unpreventable (they could edit freedom.yaml directly); the *accident/persistent-script* vector GR-101 targets is closed. *tests:* fullauto_token 5/0 (mint/single-use/wrong/expired/re-mint), autonomy `confirm_full_auto_bypasses_only_with_consumed_token` + `_gui_flag_without_token_is_refused`; audit_rpc 17/0, autonomy 13/0, neoth-gui compiles. *files:* `daemon/audit_rpc/{fullauto_token.rs,server.rs,client.rs,mod.rs,tests.rs}`, `cli/{autonomy,mod,serve_tasks}.rs`, `neoth-gui/src/main.rs`. — *origin:* review D34.

Stage 6 now re-loads the LIVE redactions and re-runs check_with_redactions under the SAME lock that applies; a now-redacted field skips the apply (records the blocked-frame audit). - [x] **GOLD-COR-27** (MED) ✅ FIXED (dead-code removal + deliberate-posture doc) — the manifest→dispatcher to_hook_stage() bridge had zero non-test callers; a plugin's declared hook_stages is NOT auto-registered into the daemon hook dispatcher. Removed the bridge + its test. This is a DELIBERATE security posture: auto-wiring a loaded plugin's WASM invoke as a PreProviderCall/PreEgress hook from a manifest line alone would let any installed plugin intercept+block every provider call + outbound message; plugin participation is instead wired EXPLICITLY by the operator via a HookDef TOML action (same gate as every other hook). hook_stages stays as advisory/display metadata. `wasm_plugin/manifest.rs`.

## WS-HR — Headroom Token-Compression Port (GOLD-HR-NN)

**Origin & verdict (2026-06-11, operator P0 "SAU WICHTIG — alle LLM calls"):** [headroom](https://github.com/chopratejas/headroom) (Apache-2.0; Python lib + a full Rust `crates/headroom-core`) compresses tool outputs / logs / diffs / files / RAG chunks **before** they reach the LLM — **60–95% fewer tokens, same answers**. Deep-read of the ACTUAL Rust core (`QUELLEN/headroom/crates/headroom-core/src/transforms/`, gitignored). **Decision: NATIVE Rust port of the pure transform logic into NEOTH** — the self-contained hard rule forbids an external Python proxy, and headroom's Rust core ports near-1:1. **The load-bearing safety idea = CCR (Content-Caching-and-Retrieval): stash the dropped original under an `md5[:24]` key + append an inline retrieval marker, so the model can always pull back what was compressed → lossy compression stays answer-safe.** Plus the **live zone**: never compress the most-recent/active turns (preserves correctness + provider prompt-cache hits). New module `SRC/neothd/src/context/compress/`; config `freedom.yaml::compression`; WAL `COMPRESSION_APPLIED` (bytes_before/after, ratio, kind). Prime integration site = the MCP tool-output path (`mcp/dispatch_loop.rs` `tool_result_blocks`) — the #1 token sink. Grounded findings (Workflow `wf_d975e4c5-136`, partial): DiffCompressor 40–90%, DiffNoise lockfile-offload >90%, LogCompressor 70–99%, LogTemplate (Drain-style) 70–80% — all gated by the CCR retrieval guarantee.

- [ ] **GOLD-HR-00** Operator tooling — install headroom for Claude Code ITSELF (`uv tool install "headroom-ai[proxy]"`) + the `headroom` Claude-Code plugin/hooks (SessionStart/PreToolUse → `headroom init hook ensure`) so the operator's own agent sessions compress context; auto-update via a scheduled `uv tool upgrade headroom-ai`. (Operator-machine tooling, SEPARATE from the NEOTH native port. ⚠ `headroom init` reroutes `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL` through a local proxy — reversible, but verify the live session survives before claiming done.) *test:* `headroom --version` resolves; the proxy round-trips a compression on a sample. — *origin:* headroom
- [x] **GOLD-HR-01** `compress/content_detector.rs` — classify a block into {code, log, diff, json, search-result, prose} (port `transforms/content_detector.rs` + `detection.rs`; the magika ML detector is OUT — use the heuristic path). The router prerequisite for every transform. *test:* a cargo log, a git diff, a JSON blob, and a fenced code block each classify correctly. — *origin:* headroom
- [x] **GOLD-HR-02** `compress/pipeline.rs` — the orchestrator + `adaptive_sizer` (pick the target size from the token budget) + `freedom.yaml::compression{ enabled:false (default), min_block_bytes, live_zone_turns }` + the **live-zone guard** (never compress the last N turns / the active tool call). Behaviour-preserving when disabled. *test:* disabled = byte-identical passthrough; a live-zone block is never compressed. — *origin:* headroom
- [x] **GOLD-HR-03** `compress/ccr.rs` — the Content-Caching-and-Retrieval safety store: stash the original under `md5[:24]`, emit the compressed body + a retrieval marker; `neoth ctx retrieve <key>` (and an MCP-visible marker the model can call) pulls back the exact original. The primitive that makes every lossy transform answer-safe. *test:* compress → marker → retrieve round-trips the original byte-for-byte. — *origin:* headroom
- [x] **GOLD-HR-04** `compress/log_compressor.rs` + `log_template.rs` — build/test/cargo output compressor (keep the 5–10 real errors, drop the noise; Drain-style template mining collapses repeated `Compiling crate vX.Y.Z` lines into one template + variant rows). 70–99% on logs. *test:* a 10k-line cargo log with 3 errors → ≤100 lines with all 3 errors retained. — *origin:* headroom
- [x] **GOLD-HR-05** `compress/diff_compressor.rs` + `diff_noise.rs` — git-diff compressor (trim context lines, cap files) + lockfile / whitespace-only-hunk offload (one CCR-retrievable tombstone per file). 40–90% on diffs. *test:* a `Cargo.lock`-dominated diff offloads the lock body; a whitespace-only commit collapses to tombstones; both retrieve. — *origin:* headroom
- [x] **GOLD-HR-06** `compress/smart_crusher/` — the flagship general structured compressor (analyzer → planning → crushers → outliers → compaction) for arbitrary tool output. *test:* a large structured JSON / tool dump compresses ≥50% and retrieves losslessly via CCR. — *origin:* headroom
- [x] **GOLD-HR-07** `compress/{search_compressor,anchor_selector,tag_protector}.rs` — search-result compression + anchor selection (keep the spans that matter) + **tag protection** (NEVER corrupt a tool-call / code-fence / XML boundary mid-compression). *test:* a block with nested ```fences``` and `<tool_call>` tags survives byte-intact at its structural boundaries. — *origin:* headroom
- [x] **GOLD-HR-08** WIRE the pipeline at the prime site — compress `tool_result_blocks` content in `mcp/dispatch_loop.rs` before it's pushed into the prompt (gated on `compression.enabled`, live-zone-aware, CCR-backed) + WAL `COMPRESSION_APPLIED`. *test:* a big tool result is compressed in-prompt, ratio audited; disabled = byte-identical. — *origin:* headroom
- [x] **GOLD-HR-09** WIRE external content — `tools/web_fetch.rs` / `web_extract.rs` output + recall/RAG chunks (`cli/recall.rs` + memory injection) run through the pipeline before prompt assembly. *test:* a fetched HTML→text blob compresses; recall chunks shrink; both retrieve. — *origin:* headroom
- [x] **GOLD-HR-10** Provider-chokepoint final pass + savings metering — a last uniform compression gate before the model call (`providers/` or `enriched_request`), token-savings tied to `UsageMeter` and a `neoth ctx savings` report. *test:* end-to-end savings counter increments; the live zone is never touched. — *origin:* headroom
- [x] **GOLD-HR-11** Upstream re-sync + attribution — pin the ported headroom version in `NOTICE` (Apache-2.0) + a tracked checklist to re-diff `crates/headroom-core` on upstream releases. *test:* `NOTICE` carries the headroom commit; the doc lists every ported transform. — *origin:* headroom

### pxpipe adoption (deep-read 2026-07-05, [teamchong/pxpipe](https://github.com/teamchong/pxpipe))

> **Provenance:** TS/CF-Workers proxy that renders static prompt slabs as dense PNGs (measured 856k→277k input tokens, ~68%). The PNG mechanism itself = **skip** for now (wrong language, multi-week hand-coded font-atlas port, no v1.0 consumer) but keep pxpipe as the SPEC if a native image-compression lane ever activates. o200k tokenizer path + CF-Workers deploy + proxy mode = **skip** (primitive-ahead-of-consumer / anti-self-contained / neoth-relay exists). The portable value = 4 algorithm patterns for the LLM-summarize compaction path `providers/compactor.rs` (HARNESS-03/ODY-06), which is DISTINCT from the WS-HR native transform pipeline and has none of these guards today.

- [x] **GOLD-PXP-01** ✅ DONE (2026-07-05) keepSharp verbatim-risk guard in `providers/compactor.rs` — pxpipe's `transform.ts::keepSharp` pattern: before summarizing any block, a throwing-safe predicate (`should_compact_block(content, kind) -> bool`) refuses compaction of blocks containing byte-exact-retrieval content (UUID/hex≥8 runs, file paths, numeric ID clusters); exception in predicate = keep verbatim. Closes the documented failure mode: summarizer silently corrupts IDs/hashes. *test:* a block with a uuid + a 24-char hex key survives `maybe_compact` byte-identical while surrounding prose squashes. — *origin:* pxpipe — *verdict:* adopt-native
- [x] **GOLD-PXP-02** ✅ DONE (2026-07-05) Semantic boundary split replacing the `history_keep_recent_chars` char-count heuristic in `providers/compactor.rs::maybe_compact` — pxpipe's `lastStaticSystemCacheControl` pattern: split stable-vs-dynamic at the last semantic boundary marker (session/turn boundary; NEOTH already has WAL session-boundary events as the signal) instead of a raw char offset, keeping the stable prefix byte-identical across turns (provider prompt-cache hits). *test:* two consecutive fires over the same stable prefix → identical prefix bytes; dynamic tail excluded from squash. — *origin:* pxpipe — *verdict:* adopt-native
- [x] **GOLD-PXP-03** ✅ DONE (2026-07-05) Compaction churn telemetry — extend the `0xF9 HISTORY_COMPACTION_FIRED` payload (`CompactionPayload`) with `static_chars`, `dynamic_chars`, `churn_detected` (sha256 of the static zone vs previous fire), `cache_hit_probable`; surfaces "static block actually churns per-turn" before it silently costs full reread every turn. *test:* unchanged static zone across 2 fires → `cache_hit_probable=true`; mutated zone → `churn_detected=true`. — *origin:* pxpipe — *verdict:* ground-truth
- [x] **GOLD-PXP-04** ✅ DONE (2026-07-05) Calibrated chars-per-token constants — replace the flat char/4 estimate in the compactor threshold math with pxpipe's empirically measured constants (`DENSE_JSON_CHARS_PER_TOKEN = 2.0` [Opus, N=391, dense JSON/code], `PROSE_CHARS_PER_TOKEN = 3.7`) keyed off the existing WS-HR `content_detector` classification (code/log/diff/json vs prose). *test:* dense-JSON history fires compaction at ~half the char count of equal-length prose. — *origin:* pxpipe — *verdict:* ground-truth (copy the numbers, not the code)

## WS-ZF — Zero-Friction / Preset-Bundles (ZF-NN, 2026-07-04)

### Companion P2P + review-fix follow-ups (operator 2026-06-27)

- [x] **GOLD-COMPANION-P2P-01** Companion phone-pairing over NEOTH's existing P2P mesh (Keet/Hyperswarm DHT + Noise XX E2E) — NOT a raw 0.0.0.0 LAN socket. *reuse (all in-repo, no feature gate):* `channels/keet_crypto.rs` (`TopicKey`/`DiscoveryKey`/`NoiseSession` encrypt/decrypt), `channels/keet_dht.rs` (`DhtUdpDialer`/announce + UDP hole-punch), `channels/keet_udp.rs` (Noise datagram framing), `cluster/hyperswarm.rs` (`derive_topic`/`spawn_discovery_with_wal`), `cluster/peer_auth.rs` (`compute_cluster_key_proof`/`verify_peer_proof` PSK), `daemon/companion.rs` (`get_or_mint`/`render_pairing_qr`/WAL `COMPANION_PAIRED`). *build (~400L daemon-side):* (1) `CompanionInvite` struct + store in `CompanionState`; (2) `spawn_companion_p2p_listener` — announce a single-use CSPRNG `TopicKey` on the DHT, accept Noise-XX inbound, verify a SECOND 16B PSK (peer_auth pattern — defeats DHT observers who see only the topic hash), call `get_or_mint`, write the chat-scoped bearer-token JSON over the ENCRYPTED channel, then burn the invite; (3) `generate_companion_invite` + QR `neoth://companion/pair?topic=<32B hex>&psk=<16B hex>&ttl=300` via `render_pairing_qr`; (4) CLI `neoth companion pair-phone` (generate invite + print QR); spawn the listener in serve only when companion enabled + abort on shutdown. Keep the loopback HTTP server for the LOCAL browser panel (dual path). Phone-side Hyperswarm/Holepunch client = SEPARATE mobile codebase (out of scope — note it). M. *test:* invite generate→burn (single-use), QR url parse roundtrip, Noise-XX token-delivery roundtrip over a mock transport, PSK-mismatch rejected, invite TTL expiry. — *origin:* operator P2P/VPN-companion directive 2026-06-27. ✅ SHIPPED: `daemon/companion.rs` `p2p::run_companion_p2p_listener` + `spawn_companion_p2p_listener`; WAL 0x0D/0x0E in `wal/events.rs`; `config/automation.rs` `CompanionConfig.p2p_enabled`; `cli/companion.rs` `run_pair_phone` async P2P listener; `cli/mod.rs` dispatch `.await`; `cli/serve_tasks.rs` `spawn_companion_p2p_listener_task` + `BackgroundHandles` fields + drain; `cli/serve.rs` spawn + `BackgroundHandles` wire. 9340 tests pass, clippy clean, docs regen'd.
